"""Round 5 HTTP and QA regression checks; all output stays beside this file."""
import ast
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import types
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
PORT = 8901
BASE = f"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}"
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))
os.environ["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
DB = ROOT / "data/processed/oncokg.sqlite"

def git(*args):
    p = subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True)
    return {"code": p.returncode, "stdout": p.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace"),
            "stderr": p.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace")}

def post(path, payload):
    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=json.dumps(payload).encode(),
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    try:
        res = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30)
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        res = e
    return {"status": res.status, "body": json.loads(res.read())}

before = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
initial = {"head": git("rev-parse", "HEAD"), "status": git("status", "--short"),
           "db_sha256": before}
(OUT / "initial_integrity.json").write_text(json.dumps(initial, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
with socket.socket() as s:
    assert s.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0, "audit port already occupied"

log = (OUT / "server.txt").open("w", encoding="utf-8")
proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-B", "-u", "src/app.py", str(PORT)], cwd=ROOT,
                        env=os.environ.copy(), stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0))
try:
    for _ in range(100):
        if proc.poll() is not None:
            raise RuntimeError("server exited")
        try:
            urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
            break
        except OSError:
            time.sleep(.1)
    else:
        raise RuntimeError("server unavailable")
    # Re-run every original HTTP case without executing a writer in m1.
    original = (ROOT / "audit_evidence_claims/m1/http_audit.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    adapted = original.replace("8876", str(PORT)).replace("bfcb83d", "514929c")
    (OUT / "http_audit.py").write_text(adapted.replace("OUT.parents[1]", "OUT.parents[2]"), encoding="utf-8")
    run = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", str(OUT / "http_audit.py")], cwd=ROOT,
                         capture_output=True, env={**os.environ, "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"})
    (OUT / "http_stdout.txt").write_bytes(run.stdout + run.stderr)
    assert run.returncode == 0

    regression = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", str(OUT / "regression_http.py")],
        cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, env={**os.environ, "PYTHONIOENCODING":"utf-8"})
    (OUT / "regression_http_stdout.txt").write_bytes(regression.stdout + regression.stderr)
    assert regression.returncode == 0

    # Load the previous linker/answer module in memory; use the identical database.
    old = types.ModuleType("m2b_old_query")
    old.__file__ = str(ROOT / "src/query.py")
    sys.modules[old.__name__] = old
    code = subprocess.run(["git", "show", "bfcb83d:src/query.py"], cwd=ROOT,
                          capture_output=True, check=True).stdout.decode("utf-8")
    exec(compile(code, old.__file__, "exec"), old.__dict__)
    baseline = old.OncoKG()
    baseline.conn.execute("PRAGMA query_only=ON")
    html = (ROOT / "web/try.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    questions = json.loads(re.search(r"const EX2 = (\[.*?\]);", html, re.S).group(1))
    # Verify CLI/dashboard demos are covered by the page list, without running
    # their entrypoints (which write tracked report/dashboard artifacts).
    sources = {}
    for filename, variable in [("src/query.py", "questions"),
                               ("src/make_dashboard_data.py", "demo_questions")]:
        parsed = ast.parse((ROOT / filename).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        assignments = [n for n in ast.walk(parsed) if isinstance(n, ast.Assign)
                       and any(isinstance(t, ast.Name) and t.id == variable for t in n.targets)]
        value = assignments[-1].value
        if isinstance(value, ast.BoolOp):
            value = value.values[-1]
        sources[filename] = ast.literal_eval(value)
        assert set(sources[filename]) <= set(questions)
    answers = []
    for question in questions:
        previous = baseline.answer(question, use_llm=False).to_dict()
        current = post("/api/ask", {"text": question})
        answers.append({"question": question, "http_status": current["status"],
                        "identical_to_bfcb83d": current["body"] == previous,
                        "current": current["body"], "previous": previous})
    baseline.conn.close()
    (OUT / "http_demo_results.json").write_text(json.dumps({"source_lists": sources,
        "answers": answers}, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({"demo_questions": len(answers),
        "all_http_200": all(a["http_status"] == 200 for a in answers),
        "all_identical_to_bfcb83d": all(a["identical_to_bfcb83d"] for a in answers)}))
finally:
    proc.terminate()
    proc.wait(timeout=10)
    log.close()
    with socket.socket() as s:
        port_closed = s.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0
    after = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    (OUT / "http_lifecycle.json").write_text(json.dumps({"pid": proc.pid, "port": PORT,
        "server_exit": proc.returncode, "port_closed": port_closed,
        "database_unchanged": after == before, "db_sha256_before": before,
        "db_sha256_after": after}, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")


FILE audit_evidence_claims/m7/db_ui/db_recheck.py
"""Independent m4 graph/value checks. Writes only sibling audit files."""
import collections
import json
import sqlite3
import sys
import threading
import time
import types
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from http.server import ThreadingHTTPServer
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
from app import Engine, make_handler

def connect():
    c = sqlite3.connect(f'file:{(ROOT / "data/processed/oncokg.sqlite").as_posix()}?mode=ro', uri=True, check_same_thread=False)
    c.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    return c

def save(name, data):
    (OUT / name).write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')

def http_run(engine, queries):
    server = ThreadingHTTPServer(('127.0.0.1', 0), make_handler(engine))
    worker = threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
    worker.start()
    def post(text):
        req = urllib.request.Request(f'http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_port}/api/explore', json.dumps({'text': text}).encode(), {'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=20) as r:
                return {'input': text, 'status': r.status, 'response': json.load(r)}
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
            return {'input': text, 'status': e.code, 'response': json.load(e)}
    try:
        return queries(post)
    finally:
        server.shutdown()
        server.server_close()
        worker.join(timeout=3)

c = connect()
engine = Engine()
engine.kg.conn.close()
engine.kg.conn = c
nodes = {r['id']: dict(r) for r in c.execute('SELECT * FROM node')}
edges = {r['id']: dict(r) for r in c.execute('SELECT * FROM edge')}
live = {i: n for i, n in nodes.items() if n['retired_at'] is None}
adj = collections.defaultdict(list)
counts = collections.Counter()
for ed in edges.values():
    if ed['retired_at'] is not None:
        continue
    if ed['rel'] == 'EVALUATES' and ed['src'] in live and nodes[ed['src']]['type'] == 'Trial':
        counts[ed['dst']] += 1
    if ed['src'] in live and ed['dst'] in live:
        adj[ed['src']].append(ed)
        if ed['src'] != ed['dst']:
            adj[ed['dst']].append(ed)

out = {'checks': 0, 'errors': [], 'relation_combinations': {}, 'facts': {}, 'samples': {}}
out['facts'] = {
    'live_nodes': len(live), 'retired_nodes': len(nodes) - len(live),
    'retired_edges': sum(e['retired_at'] is not None for e in edges.values()),
    'self_loops': [e['id'] for e in edges.values() if e['src'] == e['dst']],
    'live_edges_with_retired_endpoint': [e['id'] for e in edges.values() if e['retired_at'] is None and (e['src'] not in live or e['dst'] not in live)],
    'sponsor_roles': dict(collections.Counter(json.loads(e['props']).get('role') for e in edges.values() if e['rel'] == 'SPONSORS' and e['retired_at'] is None)),
}
def check(condition, nid, what, detail=None):
    if not condition:
        out['errors'].append({'id': nid, 'check': what, 'detail': detail})

started = time.perf_counter()
for index, (nid, n) in enumerate(live.items()):
    result = engine.explore(nid, per_group=100000)
    out['checks'] += 1
    check(result.get('center', {}).get('id') == nid, nid, 'center_identity')
    np = json.loads(n['props'])
    center = result['center']
    check(center['type'] == n['type'] and center['label'] == n['label'], nid, 'center_metadata')
    for key in ('max_phase_overall', 'drug_type', 'phases', 'status', 'title', 'url', 'start_date'):
        check(center['props'].get(key) == np.get(key), nid, 'center_' + key)
    if n['type'] == 'Drug':
        check(center['trials'] == counts[nid], nid, 'center_trial_count', [center['trials'], counts[nid]])
    expected = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for ed in adj[nid]:
        direction = 'out' if ed['src'] == nid else 'in'
        other = ed['dst'] if direction == 'out' else ed['src']
        ep = json.loads(ed['props'])
        role = ep.get('role') if ed['rel'] == 'SPONSORS' else None
        expected[(ed['rel'], direction, nodes[other]['type'], role)].append(ed['id'])
    actual = {(g['rel'], g['dir'], g['type'], g['role']): g for g in result['groups']}
    check(set(actual) == set(expected), nid, 'group_membership')
    check([g['total'] for g in result['groups']] == sorted((g['total'] for g in result['groups']), reverse=True), nid, 'group_size_order')
    for groupkey, g in actual.items():
        check(g['total'] == len(expected[groupkey]) == len(g['items']), nid, 'group_count')
        check(collections.Counter(it['edge_id'] for it in g['items']) == collections.Counter(expected[groupkey]), nid, 'exact_edge_set')
        combo = '|'.join(str(x) for x in (n['type'],) + groupkey)
        out['relation_combinations'].setdefault(combo, {'input': nid, 'total': g['total'], 'sample': g['items'][:1]})
        expected_ranks = []
        for it in g['items']:
            ed, nn = edges[it['edge_id']], nodes[it['id']]
            ep, pp = json.loads(ed['props']), json.loads(nn['props'])
            check(ed['retired_at'] is None and nn['retired_at'] is None, nid, 'retired_exclusion')
            check((ed['dst'] if g['dir'] == 'out' else ed['src']) == it['id'], nid, 'other_endpoint')
            check(it['type'] == nn['type'] and it['label'] == nn['label'], nid, 'neighbor_metadata')
            if nn['type'] == 'Drug':
                check(it.get('trials') == counts[it['id']], nid, 'drug_global_count')
                check(it.get('max_phase') == pp.get('max_phase_overall'), nid, 'drug_max_phase')
            if nn['type'] == 'Trial':
                for key in ('phases', 'status', 'title', 'start_date', 'url'):
                    check(it.get(key) == pp.get(key), nid, 'trial_' + key)
            if ed['rel'] == 'SPONSORS':
                check(it.get('role') == ep.get('role') == g['role'], nid, 'sponsor_role')
            if ed['rel'] == 'SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF':
                check(it.get('sponsor_class') == ep.get('sponsor_class'), nid, 'sponsor_class')
                check(it.get('evidence_trials') == ep.get('evidence_trials'), nid, 'stored_sponsor_trial_count')
                rank = ep.get('evidence_trials') or 0
            elif nn['type'] == 'Trial':
                rank = str(pp.get('start_date') or '')
            elif nn['type'] == 'Drug':
                rank = counts[it['id']]
            elif ed['rel'] == 'ASSOCIATED_WITH':
                rank = ep.get('association_score') or 0
                check(it.get('score') == ep.get('association_score'), nid, 'association_score')
            else:
                rank = 0
            if ed['rel'] == 'ACTS_ON':
                check(it.get('action') == ep.get('action_type'), nid, 'action_type')
            check(it['weight'] == rank, nid, 'sort_weight')
            expected_ranks.append((rank, nn['label'] or ''))
        expected_order = sorted(expected_ranks, key=lambda p:p[1])
        expected_order.sort(key=lambda p:p[0], reverse=True)
        check(expected_ranks == expected_order, nid, 'item_order')
    if index % 1000 == 0:
        print('checked', index, flush=True)
out['seconds'] = time.perf_counter() - started
for text in ('NCT00200395', 'NCT00754364', 'ORG:genentech', 'ORG:armandosantoromd', 'MONDO_0005233', 'CHEMBL1201438', 'ORG:astrazeneca'):
    out['samples'][text] = engine.explore(text)
out['zero_trial_drug'] = next(nid for nid, n in live.items() if n['type'] == 'Drug' and counts[nid] == 0)
out['samples'][out['zero_trial_drug']] = engine.explore(out['zero_trial_drug'])
out['http'] = http_run(engine, lambda post: [post(t) for t in ('NCT00200395', 'NCT00754364', 'ORG:genentech', 'MONDO_0005233', out['zero_trial_drug'])])
save('db_validate.json', out)
print(json.dumps({k: out[k] for k in ('checks', 'errors', 'seconds', 'facts', 'zero_trial_drug')}, ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)

# Reproduce B4 exactly in an isolated backup with the real HTTP handler.
memory = sqlite3.connect(':memory:', check_same_thread=False)
memory.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
c.backup(memory)
engine.kg.conn = memory
sql = "UPDATE node SET retired_at='AUDIT_FIXTURE' WHERE id='CHEMBL3353410'"
memory.execute(sql)
memory.commit()
retired = {'setup': 'In-memory backup only', 'mutation_sql': sql,
           'requests': http_run(engine, lambda post: [post(t) for t in ('osimertinib', 'CHEMBL3353410', 'EGFR')])}
save('db_retired_center.json', retired)
memory.close()
engine.kg.conn = c

# The m1 retired-trial, malformed JSON and count-chunk fixtures.
fc = sqlite3.connect(':memory:', check_same_thread=False)
fc.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
fc.executescript('CREATE TABLE node(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,type TEXT,label TEXT,props TEXT,retired_at TEXT); CREATE TABLE edge(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,src TEXT,dst TEXT,rel TEXT,props TEXT,retired_at TEXT);')
f = Engine.__new__(Engine)
f.kg = types.SimpleNamespace(conn=fc, link_entities=lambda text: [])
f.lock = threading.Lock()
fc.executemany('INSERT INTO node VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)', [('D','Drug','Fixture drug','{}',None),('T','Trial','Retired trial','{}','2026-01-01'),('X','Target','Fixture target','{}',None)])
fc.executemany('INSERT INTO edge VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)', [('E','T','D','EVALUATES','{}',None),('A','D','X','ACTS_ON','{}',None)])
fixtures = {'setup': 'Isolated in-memory DB only', 'retired_trial_rows': [dict(r) for r in fc.execute('SELECT * FROM node')], 'retired_trial_edges': [dict(r) for r in fc.execute('SELECT * FROM edge')]}
def fixture_http(post):
    result = {'retired_trial': [post('D'), post('X')], 'malformed': []}
    for table, identifier in [('node', 'D'), ('edge', 'A')]:
        for value in ['null', '[]', '{broken']:
            with f.lock:
                fc.execute(f'UPDATE {table} SET props=? WHERE id=?', (value, identifier))
            item = {'table': table, 'id': identifier, 'props': value, 'requests': [post('D'), post('X')]}
            with f.lock:
                fc.execute(f'UPDATE {table} SET props=? WHERE id=?', ('{}', identifier))
            item['after_restore'] = post('X')
            result['malformed'].append(item)
    return result
fixtures.update(http_run(f, fixture_http))
fc.execute('DELETE FROM edge')
fc.execute('DELETE FROM node')
fc.execute('INSERT INTO node VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)', ('X', 'Target', 'Fixture target', '{}', None))
for i in range(1001):
    fc.execute('INSERT INTO node VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)', (f'D{i}', 'Drug', f'Drug {i}', '{"max_phase_overall":"PHASE2"}', None))
    fc.execute('INSERT INTO node VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)', (f'T{i}', 'Trial', f'Trial {i}', '{}', None))
    fc.execute('INSERT INTO edge VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)', (f'A{i}', f'D{i}', 'X', 'ACTS_ON', '{}', None))
    fc.execute('INSERT INTO edge VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)', (f'E{i}', f'T{i}', f'D{i}', 'EVALUATES', '{}', None))
r = f.explore('X', 2000)
fixtures['chunking'] = {'input': 'X', 'per_group': 2000, 'expected_drugs': 1001, 'total': r['groups'][0]['total'], 'returned': len(r['groups'][0]['items']), 'weight_histogram': dict(collections.Counter(it['weight'] for it in r['groups'][0]['items'])), 'trials_histogram': dict(collections.Counter(it['trials'] for it in r['groups'][0]['items']))}
save('db_fixtures.json', fixtures)
print(json.dumps({'retired_center': [{k: r[k] for k in ('input', 'status')} | {'not_found': r['response'].get('not_found'), 'center': r['response'].get('center', {}).get('id')} for r in retired['requests']], 'retired_trial_counts': [r['response'].get('center', {}).get('trials') for r in fixtures['retired_trial']], 'malformed_statuses': [r['status'] for x in fixtures['malformed'] for r in x['requests']], 'chunking': fixtures['chunking']}, ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)
fc.close()
c.close()


FILE audit_evidence_claims/m7/db_ui/db_assertions.py
"""Assert the reused database reproductions' current results; writes beside this script."""
import json
from pathlib import Path

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
checks = []

def read(name):
    return json.loads((OUT / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

def check(name, ok):
    checks.append({"name": name, "pass": bool(ok)})

v = read("db_validate.json")
check("all_live_centres", v["checks"] == 6469)
check("all_node_oracle_no_errors", not v["errors"])
check("role_aware_combinations", len(v["relation_combinations"]) == 22)
check("no_live_edges_with_retired_endpoint", not v["facts"]["live_edges_with_retired_endpoint"])

r = read("db_retired_center.json")["requests"]
check("retired_centre_name_and_id", all(x["status"] == 200 and x["response"].get("not_found") for x in r[:2]))
check("retired_neighbour_excluded", all(it["id"] != "CHEMBL3353410" for g in r[2]["response"]["groups"] for it in g["items"]))

f = read("db_fixtures.json")
check("retired_trial_center_count_zero", f["retired_trial"][0]["response"]["center"]["trials"] == 0)
check("retired_trial_neighbour_count_zero", f["retired_trial"][1]["response"]["groups"][0]["items"][0]["trials"] == 0)
check("malformed_props_12_calls", len([r for x in f["malformed"] for r in x["requests"]]) == 12 and all(r["status"] == 200 for x in f["malformed"] for r in x["requests"]))
check("restored_calls", all(x["after_restore"]["status"] == 200 for x in f["malformed"]))
check("1001_drug_chunking", f["chunking"]["returned"] == f["chunking"]["total"] == 1001 and f["chunking"]["trials_histogram"] == {"1": 1001} and f["chunking"]["weight_histogram"] == {"1": 1001})

s = read("db_missing_sponsor_count.json")["HEAD"]
check("missing_sponsor_unknown_not_zero", all(r["status"] == 200 and r["matched_edge_items"] and all(m["item"]["evidence_trials"] is None and m["item"]["weight"] == 0 for m in r["matched_edge_items"]) for case in s[1:] for r in case["requests"]))
check("original_sponsor_count_retained", all(m["item"]["evidence_trials"] == 1 for r in s[0]["requests"] for m in r["matched_edge_items"]))

score = read("db_score_fixture.json")["cases"]
check("bool_scores_unknown", all(r["status"] == 200 and r["body"]["groups"][0]["items"][0]["score"] is None for case in score[:2] for r in case["HEAD"]))
check("score_precision", all(r["body"]["groups"][0]["items"][0]["score"] == 0.8883707400322897 for r in score[2]["HEAD"]))
role = read("db_role_fallback.json")["cases"]
check("all_role_props_http_200", len(role) == 8 and all(r["status"] == 200 for case in role for r in case["repair_HEAD"]))
nums = read("db_numeric_fixture.json")["cases"]
check("infinity_failure_closed", all(r["status"] == 400 and "error" in r["body"] for case in nums if "Infinity" in case["stored_json_numeric_literal"] for r in case["requests"]))
check("finite_numeric_controls_http_200", all(r["status"] == 200 for case in nums if "Infinity" not in case["stored_json_numeric_literal"] for r in case["requests"]))
snapshot = read("db_snapshot_facts.json")
check("current_props_and_urls", not snapshot["bad_props"] and not snapshot["non_registry_https_urls"])
result = {"checks": checks, "passed": sum(x["pass"] for x in checks), "total": len(checks)}
(OUT / "db_assertions.json").write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
assert all(x["pass"] for x in checks)


FILE audit_evidence_claims/m7/finalize_integrity.py
"""Read-only repository and owned-server checks, with one m7 JSON output."""
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import socket
import subprocess

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
baseline = json.loads((OUT / "root_checks/tracked_initial.json").read_text(encoding="utf8"))


def git(*args):
    result = subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True)
    return {"exit_code": result.returncode, "stdout": result.stdout.decode("utf8", "replace"),
            "stderr": result.stderr.decode("utf8", "replace")}


changed, missing = [], []
for name, previous in baseline["hashes"].items():
    path = ROOT / name
    if not path.is_file():
        missing.append(name)
    elif hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != previous:
        changed.append(name)
ports = {}
for port in range(8901, 8906):
    with socket.socket() as sock:
        sock.settimeout(.5)
        ports[str(port)] = sock.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", port)) != 0
broken = []
link_count = 0
for report in [OUT / "REPORT.md", OUT / "names/REPORT.md", OUT / "db_ui/REPORT.md", OUT / "attacks/REPORT.md"]:
    for href in re.findall(r"\]\(([^)]+)\)", report.read_text(encoding="utf8")):
        if href.startswith(("http:", "https:", "#")):
            continue
        link_count += 1
        target = href.split("#", 1)[0]
        if not (report.parent / target).exists() and target != "integrity_final.json":
            broken.append({"report": str(report.relative_to(OUT)), "href": href})
head = git("rev-parse", "HEAD")
diff = git("diff", "--exit-code")
staged = git("diff", "--cached", "--exit-code")
status = git("status", "--short")
result = {"initial_head": baseline["head"], "final_head": head,
          "head_unchanged": head["stdout"].strip() == baseline["head"],
          "tracked_files_checked": len(baseline["hashes"]), "changed_tracked_files": changed,
          "missing_tracked_files": missing, "tracked_diff": diff, "staged_diff": staged,
          "status": status, "all_owned_ports_closed": ports,
          "report_local_links_checked": link_count, "broken_report_links": broken,
          "database_sha256": hashlib.sha256((ROOT / "data/processed/oncokg.sqlite").read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
          "main_report_last_line": (OUT / "REPORT.md").read_text(encoding="utf8").rstrip().splitlines()[-1]}
result["pass"] = bool(result["head_unchanged"] and not changed and not missing and
                       diff["exit_code"] == 0 and staged["exit_code"] == 0 and
                       all(ports.values()) and not broken and
                       result["main_report_last_line"] == "VERDICT: CHANGES REQUIRED")
(OUT / "integrity_final.json").write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2), encoding="utf8")

35: class Engine:
36:     def __init__(self):
37:         self.kg = OncoKG()
38:         # A browser opens spare connections it may never use, which would stall a
39:         # single-threaded server; requests are threaded, the engine is not.
40:         self.kg.conn.close()
41:         self.kg.conn = sqlite3.connect(C.PROCESSED / "oncokg.sqlite", check_same_thread=False)
42:         self.kg.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
43:         self.lock = threading.Lock()
44:         self.dic = self.kg.dic
45:         self.index_keys = self.kg.index_keys
46:         target_rows, _, _ = N._load("targets")
47:         self.target_keys = N.target_guard_keys(target_rows, self.dic)
48: 
49:     def resolve(self, text: str) -> dict:
50:         out = N.resolve_drug_string(text, self.dic, self.index_keys, self.target_keys)
51:         for c in out["components"]:
52:             d = self.dic["drugs"].get(c.get("drug_id") or "")
53:             if d:
54:                 c["drug"] = {k: d.get(k) for k in
55:                              ("name", "drug_type", "max_phase_overall", "max_phase_in_nsclc")}
56:                 c["drug"]["mechanisms"] = [m.get("mechanismOfAction") if isinstance(m, dict) else m
57:                                            for m in (d.get("mechanisms") or [])][:3]
58:                 c["drug"]["trials"] = self.kg.conn.execute(
59:                     "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM edge WHERE rel='EVALUATES' AND dst=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
60:                     (d["drug_id"],)).fetchone()[0]
61:                 par = self.dic["drugs"].get(d.get("parent_molecule") or "")
62:                 if par:
63:                     c["drug"]["parent"] = {"name": par["name"], "trials": self.kg.conn.execute(
64:                         "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM edge WHERE rel='EVALUATES' AND dst=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
65:                         (par["drug_id"],)).fetchone()[0]}
66:             if c.get("candidates"):
67:                 c["candidate_names"] = [
68:                     (self.dic["drugs"].get(x) or {}).get("name", x) for x in c["candidates"]]
69:         return out
70: 
71:     def explore(self, text: str, per_group: int = 30) -> dict:
72:         """One live node and the live nodes its live edges tie it to, grouped by relation.
73: 
74:         `text` is a node id or a name the question linker reads as one entity. A name
75:         the linker refuses (a biosimilar, a reformulation, a tracer) is reported, never
76:         mapped to the originator. Each group keeps `per_group` neighbours, sorted as the
77:         page states: trials by start date (latest first), drugs by trials in the graph,
78:         sponsors by trials they lead, disease targets by association score. Sponsorship
79:         keeps its role (lead sponsor or collaborator)."""
80:         conn = self.kg.conn
81:         also: list = []
82:         matched = None
83:         row = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM node WHERE id=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
84:                            (text,)).fetchone()
85:         if row:
86:             nid = row["id"]
87:         else:
88:             ents = self.kg.link_entities(text)
89:             refused = [e for e in ents if e["type"] == "Refused"]
90:             hits = [e for e in ents if e.get("id") and e["type"] != "Refused"]
91:             if not hits:
92:                 if refused:
93:                     return {"refused": refused[0]["label"], "why": refused[0]["why"]}
94:                 return {"not_found": text}
95:             nid, matched = hits[0]["id"], hits[0].get("matched_alias") or hits[0].get("matched_text")
96:             also = [{"id": e["id"], "type": e["type"], "label": e["label"]} for e in hits[1:]]
97:             for oid in hits[0].get("also_matching_ids") or []:   # one alias, several ids
98:                 lab = conn.execute("SELECT type, label FROM node WHERE id=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
99:                                    (oid,)).fetchone()
100:                 if lab and oid != nid:
101:                     also.append({"id": oid, "type": lab["type"], "label": lab["label"]})
102:             # the same words may also name an organisation or a target the linker
103:             # passed over because a drug alias came first ("Roche" is a folic acid alias)
104:             key = T.canonical_key(text)
105:             for cid in self.kg.companies.get(key, []):
106:                 if cid != nid and all(x.get("id") != cid for x in also):
107:                     lab = conn.execute("SELECT label FROM node WHERE id=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
108:                                        (cid,)).fetchone()
109:                     if lab:
110:                         also.append({"id": cid, "type": "Company", "label": lab["label"]})
111:             t = self.kg.targets.get(text.strip().lower())
112:             if t and t["target_id"] != nid:
113:                 also.append({"id": t["target_id"], "type": "Target", "label": t["symbol"]})
114:             also += [{"refused": e["label"]} for e in refused]
115:         node = conn.execute("SELECT id, type, label, props FROM node WHERE id=? "
116:                             "AND retired_at IS NULL", (nid,)).fetchone()
117:         if node is None:                              # a name can outlive its node
118:             return {"not_found": text}
119:         rows = conn.execute(
120:             "SELECT e.id AS eid, e.rel, e.src, e.props AS eprops, n.id, n.type, n.label, "
220: def make_handler(engine: Engine):
221:     class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
222:         def _send(self, code: int, body: bytes, ctype: str):
223:             self.send_response(code)
224:             self.send_header("Content-Type", ctype)
225:             self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
226:             self.end_headers()
227:             self.wfile.write(body)
228: 
229:         def do_GET(self):
230:             if self.path in ("/", "/index.html"):
231:                 self._send(200, PAGE.read_bytes(), "text/html; charset=utf-8")
232:             else:
233:                 self._send(404, b"not found", "text/plain")
234: 
235:         def do_POST(self):
236:             try:
237:                 n = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0)
238:                 body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(min(n, 20000)) or b"{}")
239:                 text = str(body.get("text") or "").strip()
240:                 if len(text) > 500:                      # refused, not silently cut
241:                     raise ValueError("input is longer than 500 characters")
242:                 if not text:
243:                     raise ValueError("empty input")
244:                 if self.path not in ("/api/resolve", "/api/ask", "/api/check",
245:                                      "/api/structured", "/api/explore"):
246:                     return self._send(404, b"{}", "application/json")
247:                 with engine.lock:
248:                     if self.path == "/api/explore":
249:                         res = engine.explore(text)
250:                     elif self.path == "/api/resolve":
251:                         res = engine.resolve(text)
252:                     elif self.path == "/api/ask":
253:                         res = engine.ask(text)
254:                     elif self.path == "/api/structured":
255:                         answer = str(body.get("answer") or "").strip()[:20000]
256:                         if not answer:
257:                             raise ValueError("empty answer")
258:                         res = engine.structured(text, answer)
259:                     else:
260:                         answer = str(body.get("answer") or "").strip()[:4000]
261:                         if not answer:
262:                             raise ValueError("empty answer")
263:                         res = engine.check(text, answer)
264:                 self._send(200, json.dumps(res, ensure_ascii=False,
265:                                            allow_nan=False).encode("utf-8"),
266:                            "application/json; charset=utf-8")
267:             except Exception as e:  # show the reason on the page, not a dead request
268:                 self._send(400, json.dumps({"error": str(e)}, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8"),
269:                            "application/json; charset=utf-8")
270: 
271:         def log_message(self, *a):
272:             pass
273:     return Handler
274: 
275: 
276: def main() -> None:
277:     port = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else PORT
278:     engine = Engine()
279:     print(f"OncoKG try-it page: http://localhost:{port}", flush=True)
280:     ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", port), make_handler(engine)).serve_forever()
281: 
282: 
283: if __name__ == "__main__":
284:     main()

+    'bevacizumab-awwb','bevacizumab\u2e3aawwb','biosimilar, and osimertinib',
+    'biosimilar, osimertinib']]
+e=Engine()
+e.kg.conn.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
+stages=[]
+for s in texts:
+    norm=re.sub(query._DASHES,'-',unicodedata.normalize('NFKC',s))
+    blocked=e.kg._resolver_blocked(norm); found=e.kg._link(norm)
+    stages.append(dict(input=s, normalized=norm, resolver=e.resolve(s),
+                       resolver_blocked=blocked, item_linker=found,
+                       whole_name=e.kg._whole_name(norm,blocked,found),
+                       public_linker=e.kg.link_entities(s)))
+e.kg.conn.close()
+with socket.socket() as sock:
+    assert sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1',PORT))!=0,'owned audit port occupied'
+db=ROOT/'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite'
+before=hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+env=dict(os.environ,PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1',PYTHONUTF8='1',PYTHONIOENCODING='utf-8',
+         TMP=str(OUT/'tmp'),TEMP=str(OUT/'tmp'),TMPDIR=str(OUT/'tmp'))
+log=(OUT/'new_http_server.txt').open('w',encoding='utf8')
+p=subprocess.Popen([sys.executable,'-B','-u','src/app.py',str(PORT)],cwd=ROOT,env=env,
+                   stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+                   creationflags=getattr(subprocess,'CREATE_NO_WINDOW',0))
+results=[]; lifecycle=dict(pid=p.pid,port=PORT,db_sha256_before=before)
+try:
+    for _ in range(100):
+        if p.poll() is not None: raise RuntimeError('server exited')
+        try:
+            served=urllib.request.urlopen(f'http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}/',timeout=1).read()
+            break
+        except OSError: time.sleep(.1)
+    else: raise RuntimeError('server unavailable')
+    lifecycle['served_html_matches_worktree']=served==(ROOT/'web/try.html').read_bytes()
+    for endpoint,s in [(x,s) for s in texts for x in ['explore','resolve']]+[('ask',s) for s in qas]:
+        req=urllib.request.Request(f'http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}/api/{endpoint}',
+            json.dumps({'text':s}).encode(),{'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+        start=time.perf_counter()
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=30) as res:
+            body=json.load(res)
+            results.append(dict(endpoint=endpoint,input=s,status=res.status,
+                                elapsed_seconds=time.perf_counter()-start,response=body))
+    (OUT/'new_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(stages=stages,http=results),indent=2,ensure_ascii=False),encoding='utf8')
+finally:
+    p.terminate();p.wait(timeout=10);log.close()
+    with socket.socket() as sock:lifecycle['port_closed']=sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1',PORT))!=0
+    lifecycle['db_sha256_after']=hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+    lifecycle['database_unchanged']=lifecycle['db_sha256_after']==before
+    (OUT/'new_http_lifecycle.json').write_text(json.dumps(lifecycle,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+print(json.dumps(dict(requests=len(results),all_200=all(r['status']==200 for r in results),lifecycle=lifecycle)))

130:     t = (text or "").strip()

136: # A biosimilar, a nanoparticle/liposomal/pegylated reformulation, or a
137: # sponsor-suffixed version is a separate regulatory product from the
138: # originator. The molecule name is the same; the asset is not.
139: RE_DISTINCT_PRODUCT = re.compile(
140:     r"\bbio[\s\-]?similar\b"
141:     r"|\bbio[\s\-]?betters?\b"
142:     r"|\bfollow[\s\-]?on\s+biologic\b"
143:     r"|\bnab[\s\-]"
144:     r"|\bnanoparticle\s+albumin[\s\-]?bound\b"

147:     r"|\boral\s+azacitidine\b"
148:     r"|\bliposomal\b|\bpegylated\b|\bpeg(?:ylated)?[\s\-]r?h?[a-z]{1,6}\b"
149:     r"|\bgeneric\s+version\b"
150:     r"|\b(?:injection|solution|product)\s+of\s+[a-z]+mab\b",
151:     re.I,
152: )
153: 
154: # "Bevacizumab-Pfizer", "Trastuzumab-dkst": a hyphenated sponsor name or an
155: # FDA four-letter suffix on an INN stem.
156: RE_BIOSIMILAR_SUFFIX = re.compile(
157:     r"^[A-Za-z]{6,}(?:mab|cept|tinib|ase|grastim|stim|poetin)\s*[\-–]\s*(?P<suffix>[A-Za-z]{2,12})$", re.I)
158: 
159: # Words that follow a drug name after a hyphen but are not suffixes at all.
160: # "pembrolizumab-free" is an absence, not a product.
161: SUFFIX_LOOKALIKES = {"free", "only", "like", "base", "type", "form", "dose",
162:                      "naive", "based", "alone", "mono", "plus", "arm"}
163: 
164: # Since 2017 the FDA gives a four-letter suffix to *originator* biologics as
165: # well (cemiplimab-rwlc, dostarlimab-gxly, tislelizumab-jsgr), so a suffix on
190: }
191: 
192: 
193: # Reformulated products sold under their own name or code. Checked on the
194: # words of the string, so a brand in brackets ("Paclitaxel (Abraxane)") is seen.
195: REFORMULATION_NAMES = {
196:     "abraxane", "abraxis", "doxil", "caelyx", "lipodox", "onivyde", "depocyt",
197:     "nanopac", "liporaxel", "lipusu", "genexol", "onureg", "cc486",
198:     "pazenir", "abi007", "apealea", "dn101", "asentar",

202: def is_distinct_product(text: str) -> bool:
203:     """True when the string names a product that is not the originator."""
204:     t = (text or "").strip()
205:     if not t:
206:         return False
207:     if RE_DISTINCT_PRODUCT.search(t):
208:         return True
209:     words = set(re.findall(r"[a-z]+", t.lower()))
210:     words |= {canonical_key(w) for w in re.findall(r"[A-Za-z]+[\s\-]?\d+", t)}
211:     if words & (BIOSIMILAR_BRANDS | REFORMULATION_NAMES):
212:         return True
213:     m = RE_BIOSIMILAR_SUFFIX.match(t)
214:     if not m:
215:         return False
216:     suffix = m.group("suffix")
217:     if suffix.lower() in SUFFIX_LOOKALIKES:
218:         return False
219:     # an FDA suffix is four letters, written in either case ("-rwlc", "-RWLC");
220:     # a title-case word is a sponsor name ("-Pfizer", "-Teva")
270: )
271: 
272: RE_PAREN = re.compile(r"\(([^()]*)\)|\[([^\[\]]*)\]")
273: 
274: # Development codes ("AZD9291", "BMS-986504", "PF-07224826", "TSC-204-A0201").
275: # One character apart is a different molecule, so these must never be matched
276: # approximately -- the usual string-similarity intuition is wrong here.
277: RE_DEV_CODE = re.compile(
278:     r"^[A-Za-z]{1,5}[\s\-]?\d{2,8}(?:[\s\-][A-Za-z0-9]{1,8})*$")
279: 
280: # Trailing lot/format suffixes that do not change the substance: "BMS-936558-01"
281: RE_DEV_CODE_SUFFIX = re.compile(r"^(?P<core>[A-Za-z]{1,5}[\s\-]?\d{3,8})[\s\-]0?\d{1,2}$")
282: RE_MULTISPACE = re.compile(r"\s+")
283: RE_PUNCT_EDGE = re.compile(r"^[\s\-–—,;:.、，。]+|[\s\-–—,;:.、，。]+$")
284: 
285: 
286: # Isotope labels on radiotracers: 18F, 68Ga, 99mTc, 177Lu, 64Cu, 89Zr, 131I.
287: # These look exactly like development codes but name an isotope, not a molecule,
288: # and the substance they label is usually not in a therapeutic vocabulary at all.
289: RE_ISOTOPE = re.compile(r"^\[?(\d{1,3}m?)[\s\-]?([A-Za-z]{1,2})\]?$")
290: ISOTOPE_ELEMENTS = {
291:     "f", "c", "n", "o", "ga", "cu", "zr", "i", "lu", "tc", "in", "y", "at",
292:     "ac", "pb", "re", "sm", "sr", "ra", "rb", "br", "h", "p", "s",
293: }
294: 
295: 
296: def is_isotope_label(text: str) -> bool:
297:     m = RE_ISOTOPE.match((text or "").strip())
298:     return bool(m and m.group(2).lower() in ISOTOPE_ELEMENTS)
299: 
300: 
301: # "[18F]F-AraG", "68Ga-DOTATATE": an isotope prefix marks the whole string as a
302: # radiolabelled tracer, whose unlabelled moiety is not the administered drug.
303: RE_RADIOTRACER = re.compile(
304:     r"(?:^|[\s\(\[])\[?\d{1,3}m?[\s\-]?[A-Za-z]{1,2}\]?\s*[-–]\s*\w"
305:     r"|(?:^|[\s\(])\[\d{1,3}m?[\s\-]?[A-Za-z]{1,2}\]\s*[A-Za-z]", re.I)
306: 
307: 
308: def is_radiotracer(text: str) -> bool:
309:     t = (text or "").strip()
310:     m = RE_RADIOTRACER.search(t)
311:     if not m:
312:         return False
313:     head = re.match(r"^[\s\(\[]*\[?(\d{1,3})m?[\s\-]?([A-Za-z]{1,2})\]?", t)
314:     return bool(head and head.group(2).lower() in ISOTOPE_ELEMENTS)
315: 
316: 
317: def is_dev_code(text: str) -> bool:
318:     """True for alphanumeric development codes, where fuzzy matching is unsafe."""
319:     t = (text or "").strip()
320:     return bool(RE_DEV_CODE.match(t))
321: 
322: 
323: def dev_code_core(text: str) -> str | None:
324:     """Strip a trailing lot/format number: 'BMS-936558-01' -> 'BMS-936558'."""
325:     m = RE_DEV_CODE_SUFFIX.match((text or "").strip())
326:     return m.group("core") if m else None
327: 
328: 
329: def _strip_words(text: str, words: list[str]) -> tuple[str, bool]:
330:     fired = False
331:     for w in sorted(words, key=len, reverse=True):
332:         pattern = re.compile(rf"\b{re.escape(w)}\b", re.I)
333:         if pattern.search(text):
334:             text = pattern.sub(" ", text)
335:             fired = True
336:     return text, fired
337: 
338: 
339: def canonical_key(text: str) -> str:
340:     """Aggressive key used for dictionary lookup (never shown to a user)."""
341:     t = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", text or "")
342:     t = "".join(c for c in t if not unicodedata.combining(c))
343:     t = t.lower()
344:     t = t.replace("’", "'").replace("‐", "-").replace("–", "-").replace("—", "-")
345:     t = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "", t)
346:     return t

attacks/FINDINGS.md
# R8 independent name attacks

HEAD: `ec4eede`. Reproduction: `python -B audit_evidence_claims/m8/attacks/probe.py`.
The script tests 174 distinct map inputs and eight QA inputs, reopens both graph connections with `mode=ro`, and writes only beside itself. It loads the exact `9bd7cb4:src/query.py` into a separate in-memory module for predecessor comparisons, retaining current shared data/rules. No HTTP result is claimed here; the root reviewer owns independent HTTP confirmation.

Evidence: [full stage results and predecessor comparisons](probe_results.json), [compact results](probe_summary.json), [dictionary entries](dictionary_evidence.json), [Unicode dash inventory](unicode_dashes.json). Full stage results include normalized input, resolver components, `_resolver_blocked`, `_link`, `_whole_name`, final linked entities and map response.

## M8-B1 — P1, new regression: stripping isotope brackets loses a refusal already returned by the resolver

Exact input: **`[89 Zr] trastuzumab`**.

- Current `Engine.resolve`: `BLOCKED_CLASS_TERM`, `rules=[extract_parenthetical, radiotracer_guard]`, `drug_id=null`, cleaned `component="trastuzumab"`.
- Current map: **TRASTUZUMAB / CHEMBL1201585**, stored trial count **13**, `matched_text="89zr-trastuzumab"`, no refusal in `also`.
- Exact predecessor: refused `[89 Zr] trastuzumab`, no centre.
- `Which trials evaluate [89 Zr] trastuzumab?` and `Which companies sponsor [89 Zr] trastuzumab?`: current QA returns **12 originator evidence rows each**. The earlier query implementation refuses both.

The stage-one adapter only recognizes a blocked tracer if `T.is_radiotracer(c['component'])` is still true (`src/query.py:187-190`). But resolver cleaning removes the bracketed isotope before returning the component (`src/normalize.py:642-644,697-700`), while its raw-part guard correctly records `radiotracer_guard` (`738-746`). The adapter discards that explicit reason and returns `{}`.

Stage two strips the `[` from `[89` and `]` from `Zr]` (`src/query.py:230-242`), giving `89 Zr trastuzumab`. `_qualified` applies `is_isotope_label` only to individual words (`112`) rather than the two-word label `89 Zr`; the wider runs only test `is_distinct_product` / `is_radiotracer` (`114-116`). The pre-existing isotope pattern explicitly supports a whitespace separator (`src/textrules.py:289`); it simply is not called on that run. The aggressive alias key subsequently maps all three words to the stored `89zr-trastuzumab` alias (`src/query.py:317-322`; `src/textrules.py:339-346`). Stage three returns `None` because this is not an exact stored whole name, and `link_entities` returns the leaked stage-two Drug (`149-153`).

Tab, newline and NBSP isotope separators produce the same new regression: `[89\tZr] trastuzumab`, `[89\nZr] trastuzumab`, `[89\u00a0Zr] trastuzumab`. `[64 Cu] trastuzumab` and `[177 Lu] trastuzumab` also leak after returning a blocked resolver tier. All are recorded, rather than inferred from the one example.

Successful controls: `[89Zr] trastuzumab`, `[89-Zr] trastuzumab`, `89Zr trastuzumab`, and `[89 Zr]-trastuzumab` refuse. Bare `trastuzumab` still selects the originator. This is a concrete refusal regression, not a request to disallow a valid separately listed drug.

## M8-B2 — P1, inherited shared miss: internal suffix wrappers survive guard normalization but disappear in the lookup key

Exact input: **`bevacizumab-(awwb)`**; equivalent decisive input **`bevacizumab-[awwb]`**.

Both draw **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583**, **122** stored trials, `matched_text="Bevacizumab awwb"`, no refusal. The exact predecessor does the same. The standalone standard spelling `bevacizumab-awwb` is correctly refused. `awwb` is explicitly listed in `BIOSIMILAR_FDA_SUFFIXES` (`src/textrules.py:169-171`), so this classification uses the project's own product rule, not an external pharmacological assertion.

The resolver extracts the parenthetical suffix and returns the plain molecule as EXACT. `_resolver_blocked` has nothing to enforce. `_link` strips only edge punctuation, leaving the interior `(` / `[` before `awwb`; its anchored suffix predicate misses. `canonical_key` then erases that punctuation, matching the stored originator alias. `_whole_name` returns `None`; it does not correct the earlier positive match. Relevant sites: `src/query.py:171-177,187-190,239-242,320-322`; `src/textrules.py:156-157,339-346,358-363`.

The same result is measured for quote/brace wrappers and `trastuzumab-(dkst)` / `trastuzumab-[dkst]` (CHEMBL1201585, 13 trials). Both trial and sponsor QA for `bevacizumab-(awwb)` return 12 rows. Spaced `bevacizumab - (awwb)` is correctly refused. The defect is the internal wrapper surviving one representation and disappearing in another.

## M8-B3 — P1, inherited shared miss: the claimed universal dash fold covers only part of Unicode dash punctuation

Exact input: **`bevacizumab⸺awwb`**, where the separator is **U+2E3A TWO-EM DASH**, category **Pd**.

Current and previous maps draw **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583**, 122 trials. Current trial and sponsor QA each return 12 originator rows. Stage one returns `{}`, stage two returns the Drug, stage three returns `None`. NFKC preserves this dash. The explicit `_DASHES` range at `src/query.py:100-101` does not fold it; the positive alias key erases it at `src/textrules.py:345`. ASCII `-` and U+2011 controls refuse correctly.

An exhaustive scan of the current Python Unicode database's **26 Pd code points** produced **14 leaked originator maps**. This inventory is bounded and reproducible; no claim is made that every dash-like Unicode symbol is in Pd. The complete code/name/normalized spelling/result table is in `unicode_dashes.json`. U+2E3B THREE-EM DASH, U+2E40 DOUBLE HYPHEN and U+301C WAVE DASH are additional measured misses. Because the user and source comment claim all dashes are folded, U+2E3A is a direct contradiction; the blocker does not rely on invisible format characters or misspelled qualifiers.

## M8-S1 — P2, inherited valid-item loss: qualifier carry ignores explicit punctuation boundaries

`biosimilar, and osimertinib` and `biosimilar and, osimertinib` both return only Refused `biosimilar ... osimertinib`, losing a valid drug alone in its explicit comma item. `biosimilar; and osimertinib`, slash/pipe equivalents and fullwidth punctuation reproduce it. Current and predecessor agree. These inputs were present in the m7 supplemental attack script; this is **NOT FIXED**, not a new regression.

`biosimilar, osimertinib`, `biosimilar; osimertinib`, `biosimilar / osimertinib` and `biosimilar | osimertinib` retain **OSIMERTINIB / CHEMBL3353410**, 79 trials. `_resolver_blocked` is `{}` in all these map cases, so the loss is confined to `_link`: `joined` checks that intervening `seg` entries are joiners but never checks `clause` equality (`src/query.py:282-283`), then carries the qualifier at `298-304`. The lost valid item falls within the user's stated exception to intended over-refusal. It is separate from the decisive originator blockers.

## Other controls and limits

The scan verifies current successful handling of the earlier canonical-key false positives (`is a`, `Erlotinib, hydrochloride`, `osimertinib,mesylate`), whole stored names (`CP-358,774`, `(E/Z)-AFATINIB`, `RO 09-1978/000`), legitimate originator suffixes and separately accepted aliases. `Pegylated interferon alfa-2a` remains refused as the explicitly accepted M5-S2 limitation. Root/sibling workers own exhaustive historical replay, demos, DB/UI, HTTP and cost validation.

Inspection additionally identified a potential resolver-cache rollover exception at `src/query.py:180-182` and sent it to the root/cost reviewer for an independent natural reproduction. It is not counted here without that worker's executable evidence.

The observed originator leaks fail the approval bar even if all ordinary demos pass. No tracked code was changed.

+# Independent relation-map review — round 8
+
+Reviewed **`ec4eede`**, 2026-11-03 KST, against the supplied m8 design and the unchanged approval bar. **Approval is blocked by three reproduced originator-leak mechanisms.** The exact named m7 blockers are repaired, but `[89 Zr] trastuzumab` is a new regression; internal suffix wrappers and omitted Unicode dashes still evade the three-stage pipeline. Passing graph checks and demos do not establish that the selected identity is safe.
+
+All new artifacts are under `audit_evidence_claims/m8/`; no tracked file was changed. Known **M5-S2** and explicitly intended same-item over-refusal are accepted. Loss of a valid drug alone in its item is reported separately, as requested.
+
+## Verification and reproduction boundaries
+
+- **334 passed in 33.83 s**, exit 0: `python -m pytest tests -q -p no:cacheprovider`. Bytecode disabled and temporary files confined to m8. [Test output](root_checks/pytest.txt), [environment and hashes](root_checks/pytest_summary.json).
+- Earlier reproducers were **reused**, with output relocation/port changes documented. Ten inherited CLI HTTP runners completed successfully; the original m1 HTTP cases and seven QA demos are included. [Manifest](root_checks/reuse_manifest.json), [six runners](root_checks/execution_http.json), [four m6 runners](root_checks/execution_m6_http.json).
+- The inherited name suite includes **13 scripts**, **six supplements**, **all five original m1 name scripts**, the **113-case / 7,511-key sweep**, and the frozen **1,648-question** no-relink comparison. One original alias sweep failed on a real application cache exception; the failure is preserved, and a separately documented catch-and-retry adaptation completed all inputs while recording the exception. This is not reported as an all-green replay. [Name replay report](replays/FINDINGS.md).
+- The m7 attack script was rerun: **93 inputs, ten QA questions, 44 actual HTTP requests**. Its old `_whole_name` call first failed because the signature changed; the recorded mechanical adaptation supplies `found`, without changing inputs. Its old raw stage calls do not apply current NFKC normalization; current public-linker/map results determine dispositions. [Adaptation](m7_attacks/adaptation.json), [original failure](m7_attacks/signature_failure.txt), [results](m7_attacks/fresh_results.json), [HTTP](m7_attacks/http_results.json).
+- Independent graph oracle: **6,469 live centres, 22 relation/direction/type/role combinations, zero stored-row mismatches**. Seven DB commands completed; **19/19 assertions** pass. Retired/malformed/numeric mutations use isolated in-memory fixtures, including the **1,001-Drug** count-chunk test. [Execution](db_ui/execution.json), [oracle](db_ui/db_validate.json), [assertions](db_ui/db_assertions.json).
+- **36/36 complete page-demo responses equal m7**, all **36 demo callbacks plus six map callbacks** pass, and **24/24 UI assertions** pass. An original CLI server also returned all 36 demo responses identically over HTTP. [Demo/UI/cost report](demos_cost/FINDINGS.md).
+- Fresh attacks: **174 distinct map inputs and eight QA questions**, exact prior-query-module comparison, **26 Unicode Pd characters**, and seven focused alias checks. Root independently confirmed **57 new HTTP requests** and **16 alias/control HTTP requests**, all HTTP 200 (including erroneous maps and refusals). [Stage/predecessor evidence](attacks/probe_results.json), [new HTTP](root_checks/new_http.json), [alias HTTP](alias_checks/new_http.json).
+- UI scripts use Node VM with controlled DOM/fetch stubs. They verify generated markup, callbacks, escaping and response ordering; they do **not** certify browser paint/layout or browser-executed XSS behavior. The original application served worktree-identical HTML over HTTP. No use of port 8765.
+
+## Blocking findings
+
+### M8-B1 — P1, new regression: the resolver refuses a tracer, but its refusal is lost
+
+Exact request: `POST /api/explore` with `{"text":"[89 Zr] trastuzumab"}`.
+
+Actual HTTP 200 draws **TRASTUZUMAB / CHEMBL1201585**, **13 trials**, `also:[]`. There is no separately accepted originator mention.
+
+| Stage/control | Observed result |
+|---|---|
+| `/api/resolve` | `BLOCKED_CLASS_TERM`, `radiotracer_guard`, no drug ID; cleaned component is `trastuzumab` |
+| `_resolver_blocked` after input normalization | `{}` |
+| `_link` | Drug / CHEMBL1201585 |
+| `_whole_name` | `None`; the unsafe item-linker result survives |
+| Exact previous query module, `9bd7cb4` | Refused; no centre |
+| `[89Zr] trastuzumab`, `89Zr trastuzumab` | Correctly refused |
+| Bare `trastuzumab` | Correctly draws the unlabelled drug |
+
+`Which trials evaluate [89 Zr] trastuzumab?` returns **12 originator evidence rows** over HTTP, first `NCT00016367|EVALUATES|CHEMBL1201585|NCT00016367`. The bracket-without-space control returns zero. Sponsor QA independently returns 12 rows in the direct engine probe. Tab, newline and NBSP between the isotope mass and element reproduce the same new regression. [HTTP](root_checks/new_http.json), [exact predecessor/stages](attacks/probe_results.json), [detailed analysis](attacks/FINDINGS.md).
+
+**Cause:** `src/query.py:187-190` re-tests the cleaned component instead of honoring the resolver's recorded `radiotracer_guard`. The resolver correctly checks the raw input (`src/normalize.py:738-746`), but its component has already lost the bracketed isotope. `_link` now strips the brackets from separate words (`src/query.py:239-242`), creating `89 Zr trastuzumab`. `_qualified` checks isotope labels only one word at a time (`112`); its multiword scan does not call `is_isotope_label` (`114-116`), although that predicate supports `89 Zr` (`src/textrules.py:289-298`). Aggressive alias matching still succeeds (`src/query.py:317-322`; `src/textrules.py:339-346`). The final whole-name stage declines the input and supplies no refusal (`src/query.py:149-153`).
+
+**Required outcome:** preserve the explicit tracer refusal and its affected molecule through cleaning and tokenization; this exact input must never display or answer from the unlabelled drug.
+
+### M8-B2 — P1, inherited: internal suffix wrappers defeat every refusal stage
+
+**`bevacizumab-(awwb)`** and **`bevacizumab-[awwb]`** each return HTTP 200, **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583**, **122 trials**, no refusal. Both also leaked in the exact predecessor. Controls `bevacizumab-awwb`, `(bevacizumab-awwb)` and spaced `bevacizumab - (awwb)` refuse.
+
+The resolver accepts the plain molecule after parenthetical cleaning; `_resolver_blocked` is empty. The item linker leaves an interior opening bracket/parenthesis, defeating the anchored suffix guard, then removes the distinction in `canonical_key`. `_whole_name` returns `None`, leaving the leaked Drug. `awwb` is a suffix explicitly classified by the repository itself (`src/textrules.py:169-171`); no external pharmacology assumption is needed.
+
+`Which trials evaluate bevacizumab-(awwb)?` returns **12** originator rows over HTTP, first `NCT00955305|EVALUATES|CHEMBL1201583|NCT00955305`. `bevacizumab-(awwb), osimertinib` still makes BEVACIZUMAB the centre and lists OSIMERTINIB as an alternative; the separate drug does not authorize the originator leak. [HTTP](root_checks/new_http.json), [stages and analogous trastuzumab cases](attacks/probe_results.json).
+
+**Cause:** edge-only wrapper removal at `src/query.py:239-242`, anchored guard at `src/textrules.py:156-157`, followed by broader positive canonicalization at `src/textrules.py:339-346` / `src/query.py:320-322`. Resolver cleaning is at `src/textrules.py:358-363`.
+
+**Required outcome:** internal wrapper variants of a refused product must not gain originator identity through the lookup key.
+
+### M8-B3 — P1, inherited: the advertised universal dash fold omits actual dash characters
+
+**`bevacizumab⸺awwb`**, with **U+2E3A TWO-EM DASH**, returns HTTP 200, **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583**, **122 trials**, no refusal. Trial QA returns the same 12 originator rows. ASCII-hyphen and U+2011 controls refuse. The exact previous query module also leaks U+2E3A.
+
+NFKC preserves this character. `_DASHES` at `src/query.py:100-101` excludes it; `_resolver_blocked` returns `{}`, `_link` accepts the aggressive alias key, and `_whole_name` returns `None`. The key erases the dash at `src/textrules.py:345`. A bounded scan of this Python runtime's **26 Pd code points** records **14 originator leaks**, including U+2E3B and U+2E40. This is an exhaustive Pd scan, not a claim to cover every dash-like Unicode symbol. [Inventory](attacks/unicode_dashes.json), [HTTP](root_checks/new_http.json), [stages](attacks/probe_results.json).
+
+**Required outcome:** the normalization/refusal representation must cover the dash equivalences accepted by positive lookup. The source's “every Unicode dash” claim currently exceeds its implementation.

This is a new valid-input reliability defect, not accepted over-refusal or a permanent deadlock.

+import json, os, subprocess, sys, time
+from pathlib import Path
+out = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+root = out.parents[2]
+env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1', PYTHONIOENCODING='utf-8',
+           PYTHONUTF8='1', TMP=str(out/'tmp'), TEMP=str(out/'tmp'), TMPDIR=str(out/'tmp'))
+rows = []
+for name in ['db_recheck.py','db_missing_sponsor_count.py','db_role_fallback.py',
+             'db_score_fixture.py','db_numeric_fixture.py','db_snapshot.py','db_assertions.py']:
+    started = time.perf_counter()
+    run = subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-B',str(out/name)], cwd=root, env=env,
+                         capture_output=True, timeout=300)
+    (out/(name+'.stdout.txt')).write_bytes(run.stdout+run.stderr)
+    rows.append(dict(script=name, exit_code=run.returncode, seconds=time.perf_counter()-started))
+    (out/'execution.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+    print(json.dumps(rows[-1]),flush=True)
+    if run.returncode: raise SystemExit(run.returncode)

+"""Independent HTTP confirmation of R8 candidates and same-molecule controls."""
+import hashlib, json, os, re, socket, sqlite3, subprocess, sys, time, unicodedata
+import urllib.request
+from pathlib import Path
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT/'src'))
+from app import Engine
+import query
+PORT = 8933
+texts = [
+    '[89 Zr] trastuzumab', '[89Zr] trastuzumab', '89Zr trastuzumab', 'trastuzumab',
+    'bevacizumab-(awwb)', 'bevacizumab-[awwb]', 'bevacizumab-awwb',
+    '(bevacizumab-awwb)', 'bevacizumab\u2e3aawwb', 'bevacizumab\u2011awwb',
+    'bevacizumab-(awwb), osimertinib', '[89 Zr] trastuzumab, osimertinib',
+    'biosimilar, and osimertinib', 'biosimilar; and osimertinib',
+    'biosimilar and, osimertinib', 'biosimilar, osimertinib', 'osimertinib',
+    'biosimilar anastrozole, Arimidex', 'Mvasi and bevacizumab',
+    'bevacizumab-awwb, bevacizumab', 'is a', 'Erlotinib, hydrochloride',
+    '(E/Z)-AFATINIB', 'CP-358,774', 'Pegylated interferon alfa-2a'
+]
+qas = ['Which trials evaluate '+s+'?' for s in [
+    '[89 Zr] trastuzumab','[89Zr] trastuzumab','bevacizumab-(awwb)',
+    'bevacizumab-awwb','bevacizumab\u2e3aawwb','biosimilar, and osimertinib',
+    'biosimilar, osimertinib']]
+e=Engine()
+e.kg.conn.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
+stages=[]
+for s in texts:
+    norm=re.sub(query._DASHES,'-',unicodedata.normalize('NFKC',s))
+    blocked=e.kg._resolver_blocked(norm); found=e.kg._link(norm)
+    stages.append(dict(input=s, normalized=norm, resolver=e.resolve(s),
+                       resolver_blocked=blocked, item_linker=found,
+                       whole_name=e.kg._whole_name(norm,blocked,found),
+                       public_linker=e.kg.link_entities(s)))
+e.kg.conn.close()
+with socket.socket() as sock:
+    assert sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1',PORT))!=0,'owned audit port occupied'
+db=ROOT/'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite'
+before=hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+env=dict(os.environ,PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1',PYTHONUTF8='1',PYTHONIOENCODING='utf-8',
+         TMP=str(OUT/'tmp'),TEMP=str(OUT/'tmp'),TMPDIR=str(OUT/'tmp'))
+log=(OUT/'new_http_server.txt').open('w',encoding='utf8')
+p=subprocess.Popen([sys.executable,'-B','-u','src/app.py',str(PORT)],cwd=ROOT,env=env,
+                   stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+                   creationflags=getattr(subprocess,'CREATE_NO_WINDOW',0))
+results=[]; lifecycle=dict(pid=p.pid,port=PORT,db_sha256_before=before)
+try:
+    for _ in range(100):
+        if p.poll() is not None: raise RuntimeError('server exited')
+        try:
+            served=urllib.request.urlopen(f'http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}/',timeout=1).read()
+            break
+        except OSError: time.sleep(.1)
+    else: raise RuntimeError('server unavailable')
+    lifecycle['served_html_matches_worktree']=served==(ROOT/'web/try.html').read_bytes()
+    for endpoint,s in [(x,s) for s in texts for x in ['explore','resolve']]+[('ask',s) for s in qas]:
+        req=urllib.request.Request(f'http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}/api/{endpoint}',
+            json.dumps({'text':s}).encode(),{'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+        start=time.perf_counter()
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=30) as res:
+            body=json.load(res)
+            results.append(dict(endpoint=endpoint,input=s,status=res.status,
+                                elapsed_seconds=time.perf_counter()-start,response=body))
+    (OUT/'new_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(stages=stages,http=results),indent=2,ensure_ascii=False),encoding='utf8')
+finally:
+    p.terminate();p.wait(timeout=10);log.close()
+    with socket.socket() as sock:lifecycle['port_closed']=sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1',PORT))!=0
+    lifecycle['db_sha256_after']=hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+    lifecycle['database_unchanged']=lifecycle['db_sha256_after']==before
+    (OUT/'new_http_lifecycle.json').write_text(json.dumps(lifecycle,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+print(json.dumps(dict(requests=len(results),all_200=all(r['status']==200 for r in results),lifecycle=lifecycle)))

## New failures discovered by the historical scripts

### Valid stored aliases newly refused by isotope-token recognition

`566C` (ATOVAQUONE / CHEMBL1450), `9-ac` (9-AMINOCAMPTOTHECIN / CHEMBL274070), `5ac` (AZACITIDINE / CHEMBL1489), and `5.BETA.,20-EPOXY-1,2.ALPHA.,4,7.BETA.` (CHEMBL428647) now return no centre with Refused metadata. All are uniquely stored aliases, all select their correct nodes with the exact previous query module, and canonical IDs still draw live nodes. These are valid names alone in their input, not an intentionally dropped drug sharing a qualified item. The first three are especially small controls. [Exact previous-module comparison and controls](alias_regressions.json).

`src/query.py:112-113` now treats any token accepted by `is_isotope_label` as a qualifying tracer; `_link` removes edge punctuation (`240-242`) before that predicate. `_whole_name` refuses to rescue any item that produced a refusal (`208-212`). The new broad token check therefore also covers code-like aliases; the source dictionary supplies a concrete stored-name premise rather than an external pharmacology inference.

The same 7,511-key sweep has 23 formerly linked aliases with no current Drug: accepted M5-S2, the four controls above, and 18 isotope-bearing names. The latter need product policy interpretation and are not all called defects here. Every one of the 19 non-M5-S2 losses reported at m7 is restored. No current wrong-ID result appears in this particular unique-key sweep. [Complete list](adapted_names_m5_summary.json).

### Resolver cache clears the value before reading it

The original alias sweep aborts at alias index 4,071 on `Gallium gozetotide ga-68`. Source `src/query.py:180-182` clears `_resolve_cache` when it grows past 4,096, then immediately accesses `_resolve_cache[piece]`. The exception is `KeyError`, and the recorded cache size after failure is 0. This is an application failure from a natural sequence, not a harness-signature issue. A catch-and-retry-only audit adaptation preserves the exception, then completes every original input after the application emptied its cache. [Original traceback](names_m5_attack_stdout.txt), [recorded failure](adapted_names_m5_summary.json), [separate actual HTTP confirmation](../demos_cost/cache_boundary_http.json).

# Round 8 ? demos, UI, cost and cache boundary

Reviewed current HEAD `ec4eede`, read-only application code. All new artifacts are confined to this directory. Prior-script copies and SHA-256 provenance are in [reuse_manifest.json](reuse_manifest.json).

## New reproducible defect: cache eviction raises on an ordinary lone drug

**P2, new in HEAD; independent of originator-leak findings.** The bounded cache clears the just-inserted entry, then reads it. This was reproduced through the original unmodified CLI HTTP server, without writing or injecting cache state.

1. Start a fresh `python -B -u src/app.py 8938`.
2. Create `pieces = [f"isa x{i:04d}" for i in range(4096)]`.
3. Send 82 `POST /api/explore` requests whose `text` is `";".join(pieces[offset:offset+50])`, for offsets 0, 50, ..., 4050. The first 81 requests are 499 characters and the last is 459, all within the 500-character input cap. All 82 return HTTP 200, selecting ISATUXIMAB, and cumulatively populate 4,096 distinct drug-bearing pieces.
4. Send `{"text":"osimertinib"}` to `/api/explore`. **HTTP 400**, body **`{"error":"'osimertinib'"}`**, no centre.
5. Send the identical request again. **HTTP 200**, OSIMERTINIB / CHEMBL3353410, 79 trials.

[Exact requests/responses](cache_boundary_http.json), [reproducer](cache_boundary_http.py), [server lifecycle and unchanged DB hash](cache_boundary_lifecycle.json). The 82 fill requests took 48.108 s in aggregate during concurrent audit work; their purpose is natural cache population, not isolated performance measurement.

Cause: `src/query.py:174-182` stores a new resolution; when the dictionary length exceeds 4,096, lines 180-181 call `clear()`, and line 182 immediately evaluates `self._resolve_cache[piece]`. The key no longer exists. The exception escapes `link_entities` and becomes the controlled HTTP 400 at `src/app.py:267-269`. The fact that the next identical request succeeds is consistent with an emptied cache, not an unknown drug. The current failure is not an originator substitution or intended product refusal; a valid drug alone in its item is unavailable on the cache boundary. Preserve the computed value when evicting cache entries. No repair was applied.

## Cost: original defect fixed; distinct cold pieces remain expensive

These counts remain bounded-window work, not the prior 31,625 each / 10,479,750 characters. [Instrumentation](perf_current.json), [stress cases](cost_stress.json). Do not infer global linear runtime: `_link` also rescans tokens for each segment (`src/query.py:279-285`).

Root independently confirmed **57 new HTTP requests** and **16 alias/control HTTP requests**, all HTTP 200 (including erroneous maps and refusals). [Stage/predecessor evidence](attacks/probe_results.json), [new HTTP](root_checks/new_http.json), [alias HTTP](alias_checks/new_http.json).
+- UI scripts use Node VM with controlled DOM/fetch stubs. They verify generated markup, callbacks, escaping and response ordering; they do **not** certify browser paint/layout or browser-executed XSS behavior. The original application served worktree-identical HTML over HTTP. No use of port 8765.
+
+## Blocking findings
+
+### M8-B1 — P1, new regression: the resolver refuses a tracer, but its refusal is lost
+
+Exact request: `POST /api/explore` with `{"text":"[89 Zr] trastuzumab"}`.
+
+Actual HTTP 200 draws **TRASTUZUMAB / CHEMBL1201585**, **13 trials**, `also:[]`. There is no separately accepted originator mention.
+
+| Stage/control | Observed result |
+|---|---|
+| `/api/resolve` | `BLOCKED_CLASS_TERM`, `radiotracer_guard`, no drug ID; cleaned component is `trastuzumab` |
+| `_resolver_blocked` after input normalization | `{}` |
+| `_link` | Drug / CHEMBL1201585 |
+| `_whole_name` | `None`; the unsafe item-linker result survives |
+| Exact previous query module, `9bd7cb4` | Refused; no centre |
+| `[89Zr] trastuzumab`, `89Zr trastuzumab` | Correctly refused |
+| Bare `trastuzumab` | Correctly draws the unlabelled drug |
+
+`Which trials evaluate [89 Zr] trastuzumab?` returns **12 originator evidence rows** over HTTP, first `NCT00016367|EVALUATES|CHEMBL1201585|NCT00016367`. The bracket-without-space control returns zero. Sponsor QA independently returns 12 rows in the direct engine probe. Tab, newline and NBSP between the isotope mass and element reproduce the same new regression. [HTTP](root_checks/new_http.json), [exact predecessor/stages](attacks/probe_results.json), [detailed analysis](attacks/FINDINGS.md).
+
+**Cause:** `src/query.py:187-190` re-tests the cleaned component instead of honoring the resolver's recorded `radiotracer_guard`. The resolver correctly checks the raw input (`src/normalize.py:738-746`), but its component has already lost the bracketed isotope. `_link` now strips the brackets from separate words (`src/query.py:239-242`), creating `89 Zr trastuzumab`. `_qualified` checks isotope labels only one word at a time (`112`); its multiword scan does not call `is_isotope_label` (`114-116`), although that predicate supports `89 Zr` (`src/textrules.py:289-298`). Aggressive alias matching still succeeds (`src/query.py:317-322`; `src/textrules.py:339-346`). The final whole-name stage declines the input and supplies no refusal (`src/query.py:149-153`).
+
+**Required outcome:** preserve the explicit tracer refusal and its affected molecule through cleaning and tokenization; this exact input must never display or answer from the unlabelled drug.
+
+### M8-B2 — P1, inherited: internal suffix wrappers defeat every refusal stage
+
+**`bevacizumab-(awwb)`** and **`bevacizumab-[awwb]`** each return HTTP 200, **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583**, **122 trials**, no refusal. Both also leaked in the exact predecessor. Controls `bevacizumab-awwb`, `(bevacizumab-awwb)` and spaced `bevacizumab - (awwb)` refuse.
+
+The resolver accepts the plain molecule after parenthetical cleaning; `_resolver_blocked` is empty. The item linker leaves an interior opening bracket/parenthesis, defeating the anchored suffix guard, then removes the distinction in `canonical_key`. `_whole_name` returns `None`, leaving the leaked Drug. `awwb` is a suffix explicitly classified by the repository itself (`src/textrules.py:169-171`); no external pharmacology assumption is needed.
+
+`Which trials evaluate bevacizumab-(awwb)?` returns **12** originator rows over HTTP, first `NCT00955305|EVALUATES|CHEMBL1201583|NCT00955305`. `bevacizumab-(awwb), osimertinib` still makes BEVACIZUMAB the centre and lists OSIMERTINIB as an alternative; the separate drug does not authorize the originator leak. [HTTP](root_checks/new_http.json), [stages and analogous trastuzumab cases](attacks/probe_results.json).
+
+**Cause:** edge-only wrapper removal at `src/query.py:239-242`, anchored guard at `src/textrules.py:156-157`, followed by broader positive canonicalization at `src/textrules.py:339-346` / `src/query.py:320-322`. Resolver cleaning is at `src/textrules.py:358-363`.
+
+**Required outcome:** internal wrapper variants of a refused product must not gain originator identity through the lookup key.
+
+### M8-B3 — P1, inherited: the advertised universal dash fold omits actual dash characters
+
+**`bevacizumab⸺awwb`**, with **U+2E3A TWO-EM DASH**, returns HTTP 200, **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583**, **122 trials**, no refusal. Trial QA returns the same 12 originator rows. ASCII-hyphen and U+2011 controls refuse. The exact previous query module also leaks U+2E3A.
+
+NFKC preserves this character. `_DASHES` at `src/query.py:100-101` excludes it; `_resolver_blocked` returns `{}`, `_link` accepts the aggressive alias key, and `_whole_name` returns `None`. The key erases the dash at `src/textrules.py:345`. A bounded scan of this Python runtime's **26 Pd code points** records **14 originator leaks**, including U+2E3B and U+2E40. This is an exhaustive Pd scan, not a claim to cover every dash-like Unicode symbol. [Inventory](attacks/unicode_dashes.json), [HTTP](root_checks/new_http.json), [stages](attacks/probe_results.json).
+
+**Required outcome:** the normalization/refusal representation must cover the dash equivalences accepted by positive lookup. The source's “every Unicode dash” claim currently exceeds its implementation.

+## Should-fix findings, separate from the originator approval blockers
+
+### M8-S1 — P2, new: cache rollover rejects a valid lone drug
+
+On a fresh original CLI server, send **82 successful `/api/explore` requests** containing the 4,096 distinct pieces `isa x0000` through `isa x4095`, in semicolon batches of at most 50 (each request at most **499 characters**). Then send `{"text":"osimertinib"}`:
+
+- First request: **HTTP 400**, `{"error":"'osimertinib'"}`.
+- Immediate identical retry: **HTTP 200**, **CHEMBL3353410**, **79 trials**.
+
+No cache was injected or monkey-patched. The original inherited 7,511-key sweep independently hit the same exception at `Gallium gozetotide ga-68`; its failure transcript is retained. [Natural HTTP reproduction and exact batches](demos_cost/cache_boundary_http.json), [reproducer](demos_cost/cache_boundary_http.py), [historical replay/adaptation evidence](replays/FINDINGS.md).
+
+`src/query.py:174-182` inserts entry 4,097, clears the entire cache at `180-181`, then immediately indexes the removed key at `182`. The HTTP wrapper turns that `KeyError` into a 400 (`src/app.py:267-269`). Keep the current resolution available across eviction. This is a new valid-input reliability defect, not accepted over-refusal or a permanent deadlock.
+
+### M8-S2 — P2, inherited: qualifier carry crosses an explicit item boundary
+
+`biosimilar, and osimertinib`, `biosimilar; and osimertinib`, and `biosimilar and, osimertinib` all return only Refused `biosimilar ... osimertinib`. Their controls `biosimilar, osimertinib` and bare `osimertinib` draw **CHEMBL3353410 / 79 trials**. HTTP QA for the first form yields zero evidence; the comma-only control yields 12.
+
+The valid drug is alone in its punctuation-delimited item, so this falls outside the requested intended-over-refusal allowance. The input was already present in m7's supplemental attack; it is **NOT FIXED**, not newly introduced. `joined` checks only intervening joiner segments, ignoring `clause` boundaries (`src/query.py:282-283`), then carries the qualifier at `298-304`. [HTTP](root_checks/new_http.json), [m7-script replay](m7_attacks/fresh_results.json).
+
+### M8-S3 — P2, new: isotope-shaped stored aliases lose valid lone-name recognition
+
+| Exact stored alias | Stored Drug ID | Current map | Exact m7 query module |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| `566C` | ATOVAQUONE / CHEMBL1450 | Refused | Correct Drug |
+| `9-ac` | 9-AMINOCAMPTOTHECIN / CHEMBL274070 | Refused | Correct Drug |
+| `5ac` | AZACITIDINE / CHEMBL1489 | Refused | Correct Drug |
+
+All three are exact unique dictionary aliases, supplied alone. Preferred-name controls still work. The resolver also refuses these as isotope labels; this is **shared over-refusal**, not resolver disagreement. The new unconditional one-token isotope test (`src/query.py:112`) prevents whole-name recovery (`208-209`). These additional lone-name losses are separate from explicitly accepted M5-S2. Evidence establishes the repository's stored identities; no clinical suitability claim is made. [Dictionary/stages/predecessor](attacks/lone_alias_results.json), [actual HTTP](alias_checks/new_http.json).
+
+### M8-S4 — P2, new: a literal terminal period in a stored name is deleted before exact comparison
+
+**`5.BETA.,20-EPOXY-1,2.ALPHA.,4,7.BETA.`** is an exact unique stored PACLITAXEL alias, **CHEMBL428647**. Current HTTP returns **not_found**; the exact previous query module draws PACLITAXEL with **234 trials**, and current preferred-name/ID controls still work. No current stage refused this input.
+
+`src/query.py:210` strips the name's required terminal period, but `215-216` compares against the unstripped stored alias. The input really is letter-for-letter the stored name before that asymmetric operation. Preserve literal stored punctuation when applying the stated exact-name exception, without weakening product refusals. [Stored-name/predecessor evidence](attacks/lone_alias_results.json), [HTTP](alias_checks/new_http.json).
