+## Cost and demos
+
+M7-S3's **exact repeated non-drug piece** defect is **FIXED**. A final comparison ran after the other audit workloads finished; unrelated host activity remains uncontrolled. It loads exact `9bd7cb4` and current query modules against the same data/rules.
+
+| Workload | Observed result |
+|---|---|
+| Link `";".join(["x"]*2000)` (3,999 chars), three-sample median | m7 **1.88151 s**; current **0.16277 s** |
+| HTTP `/api/check`, question EGFR, same answer | **0.44761 s**, HTTP 200 |
+| Ordinary EGFR map queued during that check | **0.36605 s**, HTTP 200; ordinary baseline/after about **0.02 s** |
+| 400 distinct drug-bearing pieces, `";".join(f"isa x{i:04d}" for i in range(400))` (3,999 chars), cold HTTP check | **2.20771 s**, HTTP 200 |
+| Ordinary EGFR queued behind that cold distinct-piece check | **2.12630 s**, HTTP 200 |
+| Same distinct-piece check, warm cache | **0.35203 s**, HTTP 200 |
+
+Direct instrumentation confirms **400 cold / 0 warm resolver calls** for the distinct pieces, **1 / 0** for 400 repeated drug-bearing pieces, and **0** for non-drug pieces. Thus repeated-piece caching works; unique legal inputs still consume seconds while `engine.lock` covers resolution/checking (`src/app.py:247-263`). This residual cost is not mislabeled as the repaired m7 defect or a newly introduced slowdown. No timeout/deadlock or global linear-complexity claim is made. [Full reproducible cost evidence](demos_cost/FINDINGS.md).
+
+All seven original HTTP QA demos retain evidence counts **12, 12, 11, 1, 12, 0, 0**. Six complete responses equal original `bfcb83d`; Mvasi differs only in refusal metadata. All 36 page-demo responses equal m7. [Original QA comparison](root_checks/http_demo_results.json), [all demo evidence](demos_cost/FINDINGS.md).

+Disease returns **2,774 Trials + 300 Targets**, 30 each, **18,575 bytes in 72.565 ms**. The inherited **24-request / 12-worker** workload returns expected HTTP 200 shapes in **620.588 ms**. These ordinary timings overlapped other audit work and are observations, not latency guarantees. [HTTP results](root_checks/http_results.json).

+## Integrity, standards and remaining limits
+
+The snapshot has **7 retired nodes, 142 retired edges, no self-loops and no live edge with a retired endpoint**. No new wrong live edge, relation/direction label, stored field value, cut notice or generated-markup injection was established in the exercised paths. The page/docstring refusal promise still fails at `src/app.py:74-76` and `web/try.html:215` for M8-B1–B3.
+
+No repository AGENTS/CONTRIBUTING/CODING standards source was found; the supplied review/no-tracked-edit instructions govern. No application code was changed, so the conditional KOA Studio / KhOpenApi original-versus-modified-code comparison does not apply. Application paths use `use_llm=False` (`src/app.py:180-181`); no model was invoked. The application does not globally open SQLite read-only, so this review relies on inspected executed paths, isolated fixtures and hash checks, not a DB-permission guarantee.

+import json, os, subprocess, sys, time
+from pathlib import Path
+out = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+root = out.parents[2]
+env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1', PYTHONIOENCODING='utf-8',
+           PYTHONUTF8='1', TMP=str(out/'tmp'), TEMP=str(out/'tmp'), TMPDIR=str(out/'tmp'))
+rows = []
+for name in ['db_recheck.py','db_missing_sponsor_count.py','db_role_fallback.py',
+             'db_score_fixture.py','db_numeric_fixture.py','db_snapshot.py','db_assertions.py']:
+    started = time.perf_counter()
+    run = subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-B',str(out/name)], cwd=root, env=env,
+                         capture_output=True, timeout=300)
+    (out/(name+'.stdout.txt')).write_bytes(run.stdout+run.stderr)
+    rows.append(dict(script=name, exit_code=run.returncode, seconds=time.perf_counter()-started))
+    (out/'execution.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+    print(json.dumps(rows[-1]),flush=True)
+    if run.returncode: raise SystemExit(run.returncode)

+"""Audit-only final hashes, links, owned ports and verdict validation."""
+import hashlib, json, re, socket, subprocess
+from pathlib import Path
+
+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT=OUT.parents[1]
+initial=json.loads((OUT/'root_checks/tracked_initial.json').read_text(encoding='utf8'))
+def git(*args):
+    p=subprocess.run(['git',*args],cwd=ROOT,capture_output=True)
+    return dict(exit_code=p.returncode,stdout=p.stdout.decode('utf8','replace'),stderr=p.stderr.decode('utf8','replace'))
+changed=[]; missing=[]
+for name,h in initial['hashes'].items():
+    p=ROOT/name
+    if not p.is_file():missing.append(name)
+    elif hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()!=h:changed.append(name)
+ports={}
+for port in [8913,*range(8931,8940)]:
+    with socket.socket() as s:
+        s.settimeout(.2)
+        ports[str(port)]=s.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1',port))!=0
+links=0; broken=[]
+reports=[OUT/'REPORT.md',OUT/'replays/FINDINGS.md',OUT/'attacks/FINDINGS.md',OUT/'demos_cost/FINDINGS.md']
+for p in reports:
+    for href in re.findall(r'\]\(([^)]+)\)',p.read_text(encoding='utf8')):
+        if href.startswith(('http:','https:','#')):continue
+        links+=1
+        target=href.split('#',1)[0]
+        if not (p.parent/target).exists() and target!='integrity_final.json':
+            broken.append(dict(report=str(p.relative_to(OUT)),href=href))
+head=git('rev-parse','HEAD');diff=git('diff','--exit-code');staged=git('diff','--cached','--exit-code');status=git('status','--short')
+final_line=(OUT/'REPORT.md').read_text(encoding='utf8').rstrip().splitlines()[-1]
+result=dict(initial_head=initial['head'],final_head=head,head_unchanged=initial['head']==head['stdout'].strip(),
+            tracked_files_checked=len(initial['hashes']),changed_tracked_files=changed,missing_tracked_files=missing,
+            tracked_diff=diff,staged_diff=staged,status=status,owned_ports_closed=ports,
+            report_local_links_checked=links,broken_report_links=broken,
+            database_sha256=hashlib.sha256((ROOT/'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+            main_report_last_line=final_line)
+result['pass']=bool(result['head_unchanged'] and not changed and not missing and not broken and all(ports.values())
+                    and diff['exit_code']==0 and staged['exit_code']==0 and final_line=='VERDICT: CHANGES REQUIRED')
+(OUT/'integrity_final.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+print(json.dumps(result,indent=2))
+raise SystemExit(0 if result['pass'] else 1)

+"""Independent HTTP confirmation of R8 candidates and same-molecule controls."""
+import hashlib, json, os, re, socket, sqlite3, subprocess, sys, time, unicodedata
+import urllib.request
+from pathlib import Path
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT/'src'))
+from app import Engine
+import query
+PORT = 8933
+texts = [
+    '[89 Zr] trastuzumab', '[89Zr] trastuzumab', '89Zr trastuzumab', 'trastuzumab',
+    'bevacizumab-(awwb)', 'bevacizumab-[awwb]', 'bevacizumab-awwb',
+    '(bevacizumab-awwb)', 'bevacizumab\u2e3aawwb', 'bevacizumab\u2011awwb',
+    'bevacizumab-(awwb), osimertinib', '[89 Zr] trastuzumab, osimertinib',
+    'biosimilar, and osimertinib', 'biosimilar; and osimertinib',
+    'biosimilar and, osimertinib', 'biosimilar, osimertinib', 'osimertinib',
+    'biosimilar anastrozole, Arimidex', 'Mvasi and bevacizumab',
+    'bevacizumab-awwb, bevacizumab', 'is a', 'Erlotinib, hydrochloride',
+    '(E/Z)-AFATINIB', 'CP-358,774', 'Pegylated interferon alfa-2a'
+]
+qas = ['Which trials evaluate '+s+'?' for s in [
+    '[89 Zr] trastuzumab','[89Zr] trastuzumab','bevacizumab-(awwb)',
+    'bevacizumab-awwb','bevacizumab\u2e3aawwb','biosimilar, and osimertinib',
+    'biosimilar, osimertinib']]
+e=Engine()
+e.kg.conn.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
+stages=[]
+for s in texts:
+    norm=re.sub(query._DASHES,'-',unicodedata.normalize('NFKC',s))
+    blocked=e.kg._resolver_blocked(norm); found=e.kg._link(norm)
+    stages.append(dict(input=s, normalized=norm, resolver=e.resolve(s),
+                       resolver_blocked=blocked, item_linker=found,
+                       whole_name=e.kg._whole_name(norm,blocked,found),
+                       public_linker=e.kg.link_entities(s)))
+e.kg.conn.close()
+with socket.socket() as sock:
+    assert sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1',PORT))!=0,'owned audit port occupied'
+db=ROOT/'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite'
+before=hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+env=dict(os.environ,PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1',PYTHONUTF8='1',PYTHONIOENCODING='utf-8',
+         TMP=str(OUT/'tmp'),TEMP=str(OUT/'tmp'),TMPDIR=str(OUT/'tmp'))
+log=(OUT/'new_http_server.txt').open('w',encoding='utf8')
+p=subprocess.Popen([sys.executable,'-B','-u','src/app.py',str(PORT)],cwd=ROOT,env=env,
+                   stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+                   creationflags=getattr(subprocess,'CREATE_NO_WINDOW',0))
+results=[]; lifecycle=dict(pid=p.pid,port=PORT,db_sha256_before=before)
+try:
+    for _ in range(100):
+        if p.poll() is not None: raise RuntimeError('server exited')
+        try:
+            served=urllib.request.urlopen(f'http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}/',timeout=1).read()
+            break
+        except OSError: time.sleep(.1)
+    else: raise RuntimeError('server unavailable')
+    lifecycle['served_html_matches_worktree']=served==(ROOT/'web/try.html').read_bytes()
+    for endpoint,s in [(x,s) for s in texts for x in ['explore','resolve']]+[('ask',s) for s in qas]:
+        req=urllib.request.Request(f'http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}/api/{endpoint}',
+            json.dumps({'text':s}).encode(),{'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+        start=time.perf_counter()
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=30) as res:
+            body=json.load(res)
+            results.append(dict(endpoint=endpoint,input=s,status=res.status,
+                                elapsed_seconds=time.perf_counter()-start,response=body))
+    (OUT/'new_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(stages=stages,http=results),indent=2,ensure_ascii=False),encoding='utf8')
+finally:
+    p.terminate();p.wait(timeout=10);log.close()
+    with socket.socket() as sock:lifecycle['port_closed']=sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1',PORT))!=0
+    lifecycle['db_sha256_after']=hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+    lifecycle['database_unchanged']=lifecycle['db_sha256_after']==before
+    (OUT/'new_http_lifecycle.json').write_text(json.dumps(lifecycle,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+print(json.dumps(dict(requests=len(results),all_200=all(r['status']==200 for r in results),lifecycle=lifecycle)))
